# 50 — Enemies, bosses, their AI, and the damage path — PART 2 (per-family inventory)

Companion to `50-enemies-bosses.md`. This file carries the bulk enumerations that would not fit in
the main report: the **complete ability (`GA_*`) inventory per family**, the spell/asset-loading
machinery, and the verbatim dump lines for every ability class.

Method note: the two ability tables below are emitted **as markdown by SQL**
(`scripts\q\eb-30-md-enemyabil.sql`, `eb-31-md-bossabil.sql`) so that no ability name can be
mistyped. Ability names are printed as `<Name>.<Name>_C`, exactly as the dump's object path leaf.
Folder attribution = the family folder the class physically lives in.

Counts: **470** enemy `GA_*` classes across **46** families; **224** boss `GA_*` classes across
**13** families (one of which is `Shared`, holding `GA_Boss_Stun_PhaseChange_C`).

---

Reading notes:

* `GA_*Strafe*` / `GA_*StrafeSelector*` abilities are the strafe/branch pickers that a behaviour
  tree calls into. Families with at least one `Strafe` ability: `Babka`, `Brigands`,
  `CultistSpearLady`, `DungeonChampion`, `GrishaHunter`, `MS1_TwinSisters`, `MultiHeadedHarpy`,
  `Shepherd`, `SpiderBro`, `TarredCorpse`, `Vampire` (enemies); `Dwarf`, `FinalBoss`, `Hexapod`,
  `MothKnight`, `Offspring`, `Swordman` (bosses).
  `HeadBoss`, `TarredStoner`, `TheHead` and `Wraith` have no `Strafe` ability at all.
* `GA_Enemy_Evade_MeleeAttack` / `GA_Enemy_Evade_Projectile` / `GA_PredictSidearmAttacks*` are the
  defensive reactions; both are shared across many families and are family-subclassed.
* `GA_*AngrySwitchListener*`, `GA_Aggro_BS_Switch*` are the **aggro state switches** — the
  behaviour-tree side of the aggro machine, paired with `ASpartaAIController::HandleAggro`.
* `GA_AI_React_Allies_GetDamaged` / `GA_Brigand_React_To_Friend_Death*` /
  `GA_AI_Alert_Allies` are the ally-reaction abilities.
* `GA_Death_*` are the per-family death handlers layered on `GA_DeathBase` / `GA_Death_AI`.

Reading notes:

* Every boss family has a phase-change ability derived from
  `GA_Boss_Stun_PhaseChange_C` (`/Game/Sparta/Characters/Bosses/Shared/`) — see main report §3.3.
* Five boss families carry a dedicated `GA_Death_*`: `FinalBoss` (`GA_Death_FinalBoss`),
  `Hexapod` (`GA_Death_Hexapod`), `Swordman` (`GA_Death_Swordman`),
  `ParasiteGolem` (`GA_Death_AI_Parasite_Golem`), `TarGolem` (`GA_Death_AI_TarGolem`).
  Also in this folder: `GA_Swordman_Death_Attack` (an attack, not a death) and
  `GA_GolemAttack_OnDeath` / `GA_GolemAttack_Headless_OnDeath` (death-triggered attacks).
  The other boss families (`BigBottle`, `HeadBoss`, `LadyOfTheWoods`, `Monolith`, `MothKnight`,
  `Offspring`) have no folder-local death ability and must use `GA_Death_AI_C`
  (`/Game/Sparta/Core/AI/Abilities/GA_Death_AI.GA_Death_AI_C`) — `GUESS:` on the "must use" part;
  the absence of a folder-local death ability is the fact.
* `GA_SwitchLockOnAttachedSocket_Handler*` (FinalBoss, Hexapod, ParasiteGolem) rebinds the
  player's lock-on socket during the fight — relevant if a client must reproduce lock-on.

---

# P3. Spell / class-loading machinery (sync-relevant)

The spawn system never spawns an arbitrary class: the trigger preloads it.

| Step | Class | Function / field |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | `ASpawnTrigger` | `bUseDefaultEncounterFirst` `0x02E8`, `bKeepDefaultEncounterInRotation` `0x02E9`, `ForceRecipeIndex` `0x02EC` |
| 2 | `ASpawnTrigger` | `ChosenRecipe USpartaEncounterRecipe*` `0x02C0`, `EncounterRecipes TArray<FSpartaEnemyRecipe>` `0x0310` |
| 3 | `ASpawnTrigger` | `LoadEnemyClasses()`, `AssetsLoaded()`, `ClassReferences TArray<UObject*>` `0x0350` |
| 4 | `ASpawnTrigger` | `ShouldLoadEnemyClassesOnBeginPlay()` → Bool, `bShouldLoadEnemyClassesOnBeginPlay` on `AUnitSpawner` `0x02B0` |
| 5 | `ASpawnTrigger` | `StartSpawning()`, `StartEncounter()`, `bEncounterStarted` `0x0361` |
| 6 | `ASpawnTrigger` | `SaveEncounterState(EventData: Object)` — encounter state is **saved** |
| 7 | `AUnitSpawner` | `LoadClasses()`, `SpawnMe(SpawnTrigger)`, `CanSpawn()` → Bool |
| 8 | `AUnitSpawner` | `BeginSpawningEnemy(EnemyClass: ClassProperty of SpartaCharacter, Transform: FTransform)` → `Actor` (deferred-spawn begin) |
| 9 | `AUnitSpawner` | `FinishSpawningEnemy(EnemyClass, Transform)` → `Actor` (deferred-spawn finish) |
| 10 | `AUnitSpawner` | `CharacterSpawned()` + delegate `OnCharacterSpawned @0x2B8` |
| 11 | `ASpartaAICharacter` | delegate `OnSpawnFromSpawnerFinished @0xB08`, `SpawnFromSpawnerFinished()`, `IsSpawnFinished()` → Bool |
| 12 | `ASpawnTrigger` | `EndEncounter()`, `ClearLoadedClasses()`, `ValidateSpawners()` → Bool |
| — | `USpartaMassActorSpawnerSubsystem` | `ReleaseActorToPool(Actor)` → Bool — Mass-pool release path |

`USpartaEncounterRecipe` (`UDataAsset`) is the recipe payload:

| Field | Type | Offset |
|---|---|---|
| `Name` | `FName` | `0x0030` |
| `EnemySpawns` | `TArray<FSpartaEnemySpawnRecipe>` | `0x0038` |
| `HordeSpawnSettings` | `FSpartaHordeSpawnRecipe` | `0x0048` |
| `Requirements` | `FSpartaRecipeRequirements` | `0x0070` |
| `RecipeTags` | `FGameplayTagContainer` | `0x0138` |

`FSpartaEnemySpawnRecipe` — `CharacterClass FDataTableRowHandle @0x0000`,
`NumberToSpawn int32 @0x0010`, `Tags FGameplayTagContainer @0x0018`,
`bSpawnOnlyInMatchingSpawners bool @0x0038`, `Size: 0x40`.
`FSpartaHordeSpawnRecipe` — `SpawnRecipes TArray<FSpartaEnemySpawnRecipe> @0x0000`,
`SpawnSettings FHordeSpawnSettings @0x0010`, `Size: 0x28`.
`FHordeSpawnSettings` — `MinSpawnInterval @0x0000`, `MaxSpawnInterval @0x0004`,
`MaxActiveEnemies @0x0008`, `TotalSpawnCount @0x000C`, `ActiveSpawnCount @0x0010`,
`Size: 0x14`.
`FSpartaEnemyRecipe` — `Recipe USpartaEncounterRecipe* @0x0000`,
`RecipeTags @0x0008`, `Requirements @0x0028`, `Size: 0xF0`.
`FSpawnedEncounterParams` — `Spawners TArray<ASpartaHordeSpawner*> @0x0000`,
`SpawnInterval float @0x0010`, `MaxActiveEnemies int32 @0x0014`,
`TotalSpawnCount int32 @0x0018`, `bSetTargetToPlayer @0x001C`,
`bSetPlayedAggroOnSpawn @0x001D`, `Size: 0x20`.

Because `CharacterClass` is a **`FDataTableRowHandle`** and not a direct class pointer, the final
enemy class is looked up in a data table at spawn time (the row handle's table asset is a
UPROPERTY value, so `UNKNOWN` which table: property values are not in the object dump).

---

`SpartaModMagnitudeRefresh 0xb38`, `Montage BPC_MontageInterrupter_C 0xb40`,
`Enemy BPC_EnemyHealthWidget_C 0xb48`, `BPC_PointOfInterest 0xb50`,
`BPC_DamageNumbers 0xb58`, `BPC_VFXComponent 0xb60`, `Attack BPC_AttackWarningInvoker_C 0xb68`,
`AimComponent BPC_AimComponent_C 0xb70`, `NavigationInvoker 0xb78`,
`BPC_DynamicSurfacesSystem 0xb80`, `BPC_LockOnTarget BPC_LockOnTarget_C 0xb88`,
`PreAggroIdle AnimMontage* 0xb90`, `PreAggroPatrol AnimMontage* 0xb98`, `Aggro AnimMontage* 0xba0`,
`DeathAnimset SpartaDeathAnimationSet* 0xba8`, `OnNearByDeath delegate 0xbb0`,
`WBP_EnemyHealth 0xbc0`, `hasPreDeathStateType ByteProperty 0xbc8`,
`CombatText BP_CombatText_C 0xbd0`, `SubstanceFadeRate 0xbd8`, `VariantTag FGameplayTag 0xbe0`,
`PatrolPoints 0xbe8`, `LoopPatrol 0xbf8`, `OnCharacterPossessed delegate 0xd48`,
`NearbyEventDistance 0xd58`, `NearbyEventDuration 0xd60`.

`BP_AIController_C` (super `/Script/Sparta.SpartaAIController`) CDO subobjects:

(`AIPerceptionComponent`), `BrainComponent` (`BehaviorTreeComponent`),
`Blackboard` (`BlackboardComponent`), `TargetSelectionComponent`
(`/Script/Sparta.TargetSelectionComponent`).
